Chapter 113: Strange Case

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture had a gloomy expression, but he was still unwilling to leave. His Dharma image floated high in the sky, constantly scanning the hills. The two judges of the Rewards and Punishment Departments and the Head Official of the Yin and Yang Department stayed by his side.

The eyes of the illusory form of the head official of the Yin and Yang Department flashed as he scanned the area below him. The Civil and Martial judges of the Rewards and Punishment Department took out their book in one hand and their judges’ brush in the other. As soon as they found any trace, it would be captured in the book immediately.

“This demon has become quite powerful. Where did it come from? How could it disappear in only a few moments?”

The chief officer of the Yin and Yang Department was shocked, angry, and puzzled.

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture narrowed his eyes, looked over the hills once more, then looked back in the direction of the prefecture’s capital.

“I’m afraid that this demon has been performing evil acts in Chunhui Prefecture for quite some time without having been discovered by us. I cannot be sure of how many mortals it has secretly taken advantage of!”

The two judges and the head official of the Yin and Yang Department all had sullen faces and remained silent. In particular, this demon had just killed the night patrol messengers, showing that it did not take the Chunhui Prefecture underworld seriously at all. It was humiliating to be provoked in person.

The City God’s jurisdiction over the underworld was also subject to many restrictions. Some monsters and ghosts could still temporarily evade the oversight of the underworld. For those with greater achievements in Taoism, if they concealed their aura, it was also possible for them to hide amongst mortals.

However, for those evil spirits who understood just how powerful the underworld was, they wouldn’t use violent means to harm people. This was because, when a local child was born, the elders in their family would often go to the temple to pray for blessings and offer sacrifices at their ancestral shrines in their homes, and thus the birth date and the child’s fortune would be “recorded” in the local underworld’s books.

Once an evil spirit harmed mortals through violent means, there would be a bright light marking a change in the underworld record book, which would be discovered by the on-duty ghost guards, and reported to the chief officer, thus notifying the underworld government of something happening.

However, there were still some evil spirits with a deep proficiency in Taoism or who practiced strange arts who could avoid it. For example, they would slowly erode their victims by living together day and night, or more commonly by seducing their victims with their beauty to make them more willing.

At this moment, the City God of Chunhui Prefecture was a little furious. He waved his sleeve and a golden chain shot out towards the hill below. It penetrated through the rocks and soil as if it were an illusion, but to no avail.

About a quarter of an hour later, the City God and his subordinates finally admitted that they had completely lost the trace of the demon, and could only return to the city to make further plans.

“Let’s go investigate the city first. The fire that broke out just now was particularly strange. We should also do our best to thoroughly check the underworld record books.”

Even though they were mentally prepared, the two judges secretly groaned at his worlds. However, there was nothing else they could do, as it was their duty.

This so-called “thoroughly checking the record books” involved the entire population of Chunhui Prefecture’s capital city, nearly 20 thousand lives; it was an enormous task, even for spirits and gods. The Rewards and Punishment Department, and even all the rest of the 24 Departments, would have to work together on this matter for several days.

As for the Lord City God, he naturally would not have to personally participate in such matters as checking record books.

At the inn, Yin Zhaoxian fell to the ground and curled up on himself. Although the source of the intense pain in his body had faded away, the aftermath of that feeling still washed over him in waves.

Moreover, Yin Zhaoxian’s body temperature was now extremely high, and his whole body was still red. Any sweat that came out immediately evaporated. If not for a gentle force in his abdomen that continuously soothed the pain in his body, he would have collapsed long ago.

“Hoooh…hoooh…hooh…”

After a while, the pain finally disappeared, and Yin Zhaoxian’s body temperature slowly dropped to the level of a high fever. It might only have been a short while for the outside world, but for him, every breath felt like a month, or even a year.

In fact, it really hadn’t been that long since Madam Hong was thrown back and smashed through the wall of the inn, making a loud noise. Only now did people start to hear noisy voices around the inn, showing just how quickly everything had happened.

“What happened just now?”

“I don’t know, it sounded like thunder!”

“Hey, didn’t you hear a woman screaming?”

“I heard it, it was so scary…!”

“There are several holes smashed into the side of the inn here!”

“Could it have been struck by lightning?”

“Oh, it’s Yin Jieyuan!”

“Really?”

The two servants and several guests of the inn finally discovered Yin Zhaoxian. Someone hurried in to check on him. They checked his breath and found that he was still breathing, then touched his forehead and found that it was burning hot.

“Call the doctor, call the doctor!”

“Oh, and report to the police!”

……

Almost all the guests of the inn were awakened late that night. Some people had come out to check, while others lay awake and hid in their rooms. Of course, there were also a few scholars whose yang energy and lifeforce had been sucked away beforehand, and were still unconscious in their beds.

There was a flurry of activity in the inn. Some people were performing rescues, some were calling the police, whole others held lanterns and went outside to inspect the broken wood and bricks. Then, more officers arrived.

After all, the list of top examinees had just been announced, and another prefecture’s top scorer was suspected of having been attacked, which was definitely no trivial matter.

Invisible to the the naked eye, on the high roofs of the inn and surrounding buildings, the City God of Chunhui Prefecture and his subordinate officials gazed down on the scene. Many underworld officials were investigating the case just like their counterpart officials in the world of the living. The foundations of the security of two worlds, the Yin and the Yang, worked side by side without either side being aware.

“Just now, a flame burst out and repelled the demon, but there is not even a single scorch mark on the curtains of the inn. It’s very strange!”

The City God’s vision seemed to penetrate through the roof to see the current state of Yin Zhaoxian’s original room in the inn. Then, he turned his eyes to look at Yin Zhaoxian, who had already been moved to another place.

“It is rare for an ordinary scholar to harbor a trace of righteous energy in his mind, and it is very easy for it to dissipate. However, this person’s virtue is so strong that it can actually be manifested. How rare!”

“Your Excellency is correct, but he is only a mortal. It would be impossible for him to have the kind of strength to repel such a powerful demon.”

The head of the Yin and Yang Department had already visited Yin Zhaoxian, and knew that he practiced neither martial arts nor any cultivation practices for spiritual energy. Of course, Yin Zhaoxian himself was extremely talented, and had a profoundly dignified personality, and possessed a noble aura, which made him unusually extraordinary.

“This is a man of great integrity. Perhaps he saw some traces of that demon. I will personally send myself to him in a dream later, and ask him what he has seen!”

After the City God finished speaking, he turned around and returned to the temple with his officials.

Although the cultivation techniques of sending dreams and entering dreams seemed to be similar, they were in fact two different techniques. The former could be regarded as a gift from gods and spirits, while the latter was a special technique that had to be performed by an immortal cultivator.

In the inn, the doctor diagnosed Yin Zhaoxian with a simple cold.1 He told him to keep warm, prescribed some medicine to help him sweat it out, and mentioned that the symptoms, which had turned into a low-grade fever, would soon subside. Yin Zhaoxian stayed awake for a while after the doctor visited, though he soon succumbed to exhaustion and fell asleep again. The officers and the innkeeper breathed a sigh of relief.

When Yin Zhaoxian was awake, he only informed the officers that he was attacked by a woman in red who called herself Madam Hong. He did not divulge that what he had seen was a monster. He did not want to be regarded as an insane scholar.

It was now the time of the the fourth watch. After the night watchman had rung his gong for a while, a gust of cold wind and the scent of incense blew into the guest room where Yin Zhaoxian was staying. However, compared to other manifestations, the presence of the City God was not accompanied by a gloomy feeling.

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture glanced at the inn servant in charge of guarding Yin Zhaoxian’s rooms, who was dozing on the table. Then he looked at Yin Zhaoxian, waved his arm, and his figure disappeared in a blur.

Yin Zhaoxian, who was sleeping on the bed, immediately frowned.

In his dream, he found himself sitting in a dimly lit room, with a bed underneath him. Before him stood a man, with half of his body shrouded in darkness. He could not see him clearly, but he looked like a high-ranking official of the imperial court. The man was sitting on a stool in front of a table.

The more Yin Zhaoxian tried to see clearly, the blurrier it seemed. The other party exuded a heavy aura.

“Where am I? Sir, who are you?”

“Do not be afraid, Yin Jieyuan.2 I am the City God of Chunhui Prefecture. I have come to ask you a question!”

The voice of the City God was like a loud bell, ringing out in front of him and echoing all around, calming Yin Zhaoxian.

After all, people’s thoughts in their dreams were often unclear, their emotions were amplified, and sometimes they did some stupid or illogical things.

When Yin Zhaoxian heard that the other party was the City God, he was shocked, and hurriedly cupped his hands and bowed.

“I, Yin Zhaoxian, greet the Lord City God!”

“Well, Yin Jieyuan, let me ask you, did you see the face of the woman who attacked you in the inn?”

It was not easy for him to tell others about these events, but he had to tell the City God the truth. At this moment, Yin Zhaoxian was under the impression that it was most likely the City God who had saved him when he was almost killed by the demon.

“Many thanks to you, Lord City God, for saving me during my crisis. The woman just now was no mortal. I vaguely saw that she looked like a bloody skeleton. She called herself ‘Madam Hong’.”

The City God frowned.

“Madam Hong?”

“That is correct. As for the rest, I, Yin, am unaware. At that time, I was really dizzy and in great pain.”

The City God smiled. He could easily imagine the scene in which an ordinary person, who had never seen a ghost in his life, encountered a demon. Fortunately, this man had a righteous spirit.

However, the City God suddenly thought of the flames from before, and realized that this Yin Zhaoxian might have previously had some other adventure.

“Oh, there is one more thing. Yin Jieyuan, when the demon was about to harm you, a strange flame appeared and repelled it. However, nothing in the inn was burnt. Did some master gift you with some protective item? “

When the City God asked this question, Yin Zhaoxian realized that it was not him who had saved him in the crisis just now. The first thought in his mind was of Ji Yuan, and then of the old man who had swallowed half a tree’s worth of jujubes the other night.

“To tell you the truth, I am fortunate enough to have made the acquaintance of a friend who has the ability to make ghosts, gods, and monsters admire him. He is definitely no ordinary person. When we parted, he gave me a letter as a gift… But I dare not reveal his name without his consent…”

The City God nodded slightly. It was fine as long as he understood the reason.

“It must be this person who protected you. He must have seen Yin Jieyuan’s righteousness at the time and helped you…”

The City God pondered for a moment and realized that Yin Zhaoxian would not be able to give him any more information.

“Thank you, Yin Jieyuan, for clearing up my confusion. I will take my leave!”

In the dream, the City God bowed slightly, and Yin Zhaoxian hurriedly returned the salute. In a trance, the dream ended, and Yin Zhaoxian fell back into sleep.

T/n: …Alright who’s that doctor because someone is clearly a quack. The man literally has 1st or 2nd degree burns on his body and you’re saying “oh, yeah, sure, it’s a cold”

T/n: you have no idea how tempted I was to put “BE NOT AFRAID”

Chapter 114: Edict – “Slay the Demon”

Ji Yuan, currently in Juntian Prefecture, stayed up the entire night in his inner world to carefully guard the white chess piece that belonged to Yin Zhaoxian. Once the chess piece absorbed a little of the red Qi covering its surface, Ji Yuan would immediately replenish it.

He was so careful because the heat on the white piece had not subsided.

In fact, Ji Yuan knew that it would not be very useful for him to just sit there and watch, and that the chess piece would be able to go to the alchemy furnace on its own to absorb the overflowing red Qi.

But after all, it was his best and only friend in his life who had just suffered an accident, so he wouldn’t be able to sleep anyway. Fortunately, as he waited, he also worked hard to refine the energy of the five elements and the spiritual energy in his Dantian.

Although the old flood dragon whom he had met that year was nominally Ji Yuan’s friend, their relationship was not that deep, as they had only chatted once.

The white piece’s heat did not subside until dawn, when the warmth finally stopped coming back, and Ji Yuan heaved a sigh of relief.

Ji Yuan, who had been sitting cross-legged all this time, exited his inner world and lay back on the bed.

Then, he thought back to the most anxiety-inducing situation of the night, and laughed at himself.

“This True Fire of Samadhi… is really fierce…”

Ji Yuan’s face grew serious after he had made fun of himself.

“Master Yin, Master Yin, what on earth did you get into…”

In the moment when the True Fire of Samadhi collided with Madam Hong, Ji Yuan could also feel the demonic energy and could even vaguely hear a resentful scream.

The other party obviously hated Yin Zhaoxian. Even though it later attracted the City God of Chunhui Prefecture, Ji Yuan could not be certain that the City God had captured the demon.

‘What if he failed to capture it? ’

Ji Yuan narrowed his eyes. As the saying went, distant water wouldn’t quench immediate thirst.1 He and Master Yin were separated by an entire province, which was a distance of over than two thousand li in a straight line, not to mention the mountains, rivers, and various complex terrains in between. In addition, Ji Yuan might be a little directionally challenged,2 so it would be difficult for him to get there in a short period of time.

This True Fire of Samadhi technique could only be used once, and never a second time. Otherwise, Master Yin may very well be refined to death by the true fire in the furnace even before the demon died. And the next time, the demon would definitely be on guard.

However, that demon was quite powerful, and even if Ji Yuan were there in person, he would not dare to confront it. It had only been seriously injured by the True Fire of Samadhi when it had been caught off guard.

Although it had only been swept away by the flame and was not actually burned, that flame was no ordinary fire, but was the flame of the infinite True Fire of his alchemy furnace. Being burned by it would definitely not be pleasant.

Ji Yuan kept thinking of countermeasures. He wanted to ensure that his best friend would be safe. It would be best if he could execute the demon. After all, Master Yin was not only an important piece for the sake of humanity, but also Ji Yuan’s best friend.

‘Execute?’3

Ji Yuan’s own thoughts suddenly made him realize something, and he turned his gaze to his bedside.

Now that he was traveling, the Green Vine Immortal Sword had been floating behind him, hidden.

Because the immortal sword had already formed a sword spirit, the sword was able to conceal its own body. Nor only could ordinary people not see it, but even immortals and demons who had not reached a sufficient level of cultivation would not be able to sense it. It was like the books in Heavenly Script.

In a sense, Heavenly Script could be regarded as a kind of text that imitated the formation of a spirit, so the immortal sword, which itself grew a spirit, would naturally find it easier to perform similar actions, and would be more active in performing them. Apart from its master, few people could see it. This, along with the ability of flight, could be regarded as one of the magical abilities granted to the Green Vine Sword after it had formed a sword spirit.

Seeing Ji Yuan looking at him, the Green Vine Sword beside the bed floated up and flew to Ji Yuan’s side.

‘Although I may not be strong enough to face that kind of demon out of the blue, I have the Green Vine Sword! If I prepare for it well, I may have a chance!’

With this thought, Ji Yuan sat up again and drew the Green Vine Sword out of its scabbard, without releasing any of its sword light.

“It’s up to you this time!”

Hum…

Although it didn’t quite understand what its master meant, the Green Vine Sword still responded.

Ji Yuan took a deep breath, and captured the sound of the word “Edict” in his mouth. The mysterious yellow energy in his inner world flowed out along the golden bridge, guided by Ji Yuan’s spiritual energy to the fingertips of his right hand.

His fingers seemed to weigh thousands of pounds, and his fingertips bled red.

Ji Yuan made sword fingers out of his right hand and pointed them at the sword. The mysterious yellow energy surged out alongside his spiritual energy and blood, and he repeatedly wrote one single word in seal script.

“Execute, execute, execute, execute…”

After each character was written, the next would become even more difficult to write, and would consume even more energy. In the end, the energy of his Dantian was completely used up, and he had to convert part of his spiritual energy into red Qi4 to write the next character, and so on for the ones after that.

By the time he had finished writing all of the repetitions of the character, the body of the sword held forty-nine “execute” characters. Ji Yuan was already pale and dizzy, and it was already three -quarters-of-an-hour past noon.

As the last “execute” fell, Ji Yuan forcibly resisted the dizziness, and guided a strand of Yang energy from Heaven and Earth to seal it within the Green Vine Sword’s body.

The bloodied characters drawn from mysterious yellow energy on the Green Vine Sword gradually disappeared in a flash of light, restoring the original appearance of the sword.

“Hooh…hoo…hooh…”

Ji Yuan leaned against the wall beside his bed, feeling drained. He touched his numb and itching nose with his hands. There was indeed a trace of blood.

The Green Vine Sword had been accumulating energy for three years, and it still held tremendous power. Its expenditure in cutting wild fruits on the mountain was negligible. Ji Yuan used the mysterious yellow Qi to issue a decree, writing the word “execute” 49 times in a row, determined to combine it with the power of the Green Vine Sword to kill the wounded demon.

After all, it was a powerful, transformed monster. Ji Yuan had no idea how much he needed to do, so he used all of his tricks.

However, although he was not sure whether it would work, during the writing process Ji Yuan focused on being determined to kill the being, without any mental fluctuations, in order to build up momentum for the Green Vine Sword.

The Green Vine Sword was now suspended in front of the bed, neither making a sound nor flying around. It seemed as if it were being pressed down by a heavy aura, keeping its sharp edge hidden and sealed away.

When Ji Yuan recovered a little, he grabbed the scabbard and slowly sheathed the Green Vine Sword. Then he walked to the bed in the guest room, propped open the wooden, turned around, and instructed the Green Vine Sword:

“The flame of the True Fire of Samadhi is not so easy to bear. Go to Master Yin quickly and use the trace of the True Fire to track down the evil spirit and execute it! Remember, it is a transformed monster. You can only use one sword strike at your peak strength, and then your power will decline. If you fail in one strike, you must not continue fighting!”

Buzz…

The sound of the Green Vine Sword was subtle but powerful, and its restrained sword intent blurred the air within two inches of the sword.

“Alright, go on!”

Ji Yuan’s tired voice finished.

Swoosh…

The Green Vine Sword turned into a stream of light and flew out the wooden window, soaring into the sky. In less than a dozen breaths, it had risen to a high altitude, and traveled rapidly along the wind. Its blade pointed towards Chunhui Prefecture in Ji Province in the distance. Its speed was actually beyond Ji Yuan’s imagination.

Spirits were difficult to conceive, and difficult to form; the underlying principles of fate were as mysterious and profound as the practice of enlightenment. There were only few famous immortal artifacts and spiritual treasures in the entire world of immortal cultivation. The reason why Ji Yuan, who had always been conservative when measuring power, determined that it was an immortal sword was because the sword spirit had already arisen, and it was intelligent and fully formed.

Ji Yuan looked at his immortal sword flying into the sky and stopped worrying about whether it would succeed. He muttered to himself.

“I’m a little envious…”

Another burst of dizziness came over him. Ji Yuan shook his head, returned to the bed, and sat cross-legged. He used the Qi Guiding Technique to gather spiritual energy.

As waves of spiritual energy converged to nourish his body, Ji Yuan’s discomfort gradually faded away.

T/n: the bromance is strong in this one

远水解不了近渴: Chinese proverb, implies the need to come up with an immediate solution to a problem – a vague future solution won’t work.

T/n: Well at least he’s starting to be self-aware

斩 (zhǎn): to behead (capital punishment), to chop. This word comes up a LOT (similar to “edict”), and might also be translated as “cut” or “behead.” I translated it as “execute” because of the connotations and how it’s later used.

If you will recall, this is specifically the refined Qi in the Dantian

Chapter 115: Immortal Sword Suspended in the Air, Dark Wind Trembles

[T/n: note on chapter title]1

At midnight on the previous day.

Wafeng2 Mountain sits at the junction of Chunhui Prefecture and Duming Prefecture in Ji Province. In the mountain is a deep ravine named Dark Wind3 Ravine, which received its name due to how difficult it was to see the sunlight from the ravine, and how the mountain winds blew through it all year round.

Even if the locals entered the mountain, they would never set foot there, because if they accidentally slipped into Dark Wind Ravine, it would be difficult to climb back out; many mountaineers had thus lost their lives.

That night, a faint yellow glow flowed from inside Dark Wind Ravine. Madam Hong staggered out of the soil, and a yellow talisman in her left hand turned to ashes.

“Haaah…haah…”

She raised her hand and looked at her right arm with, hands trembling. The nails had been completely scorched and curled up, and the entire right arm below the nails had also been completely charred. The skeleton was clearly exposed in many spots, and several other places on her body were charred black. Her emotions were even more heart-wrenching.

“Madam Hong? What’s wrong?”

As if sensing the chaotic demonic energy, a figure appeared, staring at the embarrassed Madam Hong in astonishment.

“Haah…haah…I not careful enough, I got caught…Hurry and help me in…”

The newcomer did not dare to delay, and hurriedly helped Madam Hong into a cave below Dark Wind Ravine. Inside the cave was another mystery.

There was an open space in the mountainside filled with a blood red air. Although there were no pavilions or terraces, there were stone tables, stone chairs, and carpets scattered throughout. There were also various rooms in the wall along the winding cave paths.

“Huh?”

“Madam Hong?”

“How did this happen?”

“The City God of Chunhui Prefecture actually has such a technique?”

“Could it be that the old flood dragon took action?”

“Impossible, if that old flood dragon took action… Haha, Madam Hong might not have been able to make it back!”

Feeling Madam Hong’s aura, several surprised voices sounded in the cave. All of them were astonished at her serious injury.

“Ah… stop guessing. This injury was partly caused by the City God of Chunhui Prefecture, but the main reason was that I was caught off guard by a mortal scholar!”

“Mortal?”

“That’s right, that scholar’s righteous spirit had become quite impressive. I think he must have learned some self-defense skills from a Taoist master. I was greedy and didn’t check, and got burned by some kind of fire…”

As she recalled that kind of firepower, Madam Hong was also a little scared. However, although the fire was terrifying, it was not impossible for her to resist. Even if she was caught off guard and hit by its full power, she was only slightly injured. It was just that the painful burning sensation was unbearable, and it still lingered.

However, for a moment, Madam Hong had sensed a vague illusion, as if what she had been struck by was nothing more than a ray of flame on the periphery of a raging sea of ​​​​fire.

Noticing a couple demons around her looking at her from the entrances of their respective rooms, some reserved and others more arrogant, Madam Hong was embarrassed and a little upset. Their inquiries were not friendly at all. She asked the man who was helping support her:

“I’ll take my leave and go to the inner chamber at the back to heal my wounds. Is there any blood in the cave?”

At this moment, because of the faint bloody light in the cave, which was only slightly brighter than the Dark Wind Ravine outside, the man actually only displayed half of his face. The lower half of his face was covered in fur, like some kind of fanged beast.

“There are four more, all bought from gangs in the martial arts world in Dingyuan Prefecture. Two of them are mortal warriors with relatively strong vitality and Qi.”

Taking advantage of the greed and ignorance of mortals was also a method to avoid inspections by the earthly gods and spirits. Obviously, these monsters were no longer ignorant spirits who practiced hard in the mountains and cultivated to seek enlightenment; this was not the first time they had done this kind of thing.

“Yes, send them all to my alcove. When I recover from my wounds, I will personally find new blood food.”

When Madam Hong heard that there were still four, she felt relieved.

“Haha, as Madam Hong wishes, of course it’s fine!”

The man supported Madam Hong, who used to appear so graceful but was now in a miserable state, to the depths of the cave. He watched her enter a cave room that smelled of rouge powder before leaving.

Deep down another fork in the demon cave was a stone chamber. Behind the heavy stone door, several young men and women with desperate expressions huddled dejectedly in the corner.

Originally, there had been twenty people imprisoned here together, but after just half a month, only a few of them were left.

There was no food and only two buckets of water every day, but hunger was already only secondary. Fear was the root of their daily torment. They had long realized that they had been captured by a demon, the kind of demon that was only thought to exist in the stories that the older generation would tell, that would eat people without batting an eye.

The people who had already been taken away never came back, and no sounds could be heard, but there was no need to wonder what their fate might be.

However, not all of them died at the hands of demons. Three of them had taken their own lives because they could not bear the constant fear.

“Tick…tick…tick…”

The sound of water dripping from the stalactites made the dim stone chamber feel even more deadly and emphasized the silence.

The sound of footsteps approached in the distance. The four people in the stone room suddenly grew nervous, and their breathing trembled slightly.

“Demon… The demon is coming… the demon is coming!”

“Wuuu… Wuuu… Wuuu…”

“Oh, I don’t want to die…”

The demon with a half-human face walked into the stone chamber and peered inside. He could smell a strong odor of urine; it wasn’t necessarily that someone was incontinent, but rather because the stone room was closed, and there was no place for daily excretion.

“Haha…”

The demon laughed, stretched out a hand, and pushed. The stone door slowly swung open, making a grating sound of friction against the stones on the ground, accompanied by a popping burst of sparks.

“Don’t be afraid anymore, you will be free today…”

Glancing at the two warrior men, the half-human-faced demon grinned again.

“The two of you might even be able to enjoy some bliss before you die… Well, that seems unlikely today…”

The half-human-faced demon thought of Madam Hong’s condition; he was afraid she would not be in a good mood today.

Not too long after, the four young men and women who had already fainted arrived at Madam Hong’s alcove. Looking at the four young faces, Madam Hong could hardly wait for the half-human-faced man to leave, before she moved in close.

“Hiss…”

Madam Hong opened her mouth and inhaled, the sound gradually growing louder and louder. A blood-colored mist floated out of the seven orifices4 of each of the four individuals. The limbs of the four unconscious victims were twitching on the ground. Their skin gradually shriveled up, and their struggling movements weakened.

After about the time it took to drink a cup of tea, she had completely turned them into four mummified corpses, and a thick blood-colored mist filled the interior of Madam Hong’s room.

Lying on a stone bed covered with animal skins, Madam Hong began to breathe in and out slowly. Wisps of spiritual energy from Dark Wind Ravine slowly gathered in the cave and floated into Madam Hong’s room.

The spiritual energy mixed with the blood-colored mist and flowed into Madam Hong’s body through her small, crimson-colored mouth as she inhaled once more. Blood began to visibly spread through the charred half of her body.

Dawn rose on Wafeng Mountain, and the angle of the sun rose higher and higher as time passed, but the depths of the ravine were still dark.

It was not until late in the afternoon that Madam Hong finally stopped her regeneration. The blood-colored mist in the cave had been completely consumed, and she had barely suppressed the burning pain; rather, she had barely been able to endure the pain.

It’s just that for some reason, a great uneasiness arose in her heart, as if some disaster were imminent!

“Could it be related to this fire injury?”

After all, she was a skeleton blood demon who had been practicing for a long time. Although her evil spirit had been blinded to the way of heaven, she still had a vague premonition.

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture could not see though the Hidden Earth Escape Talisman,5 so he must not have been the one to prompt this feeling. And the old flood dragon never cared about things outside of his river; plus, even though he was a deity, he was still a type of monster. What was more, the old flood dragon might not even know anything about the current happenings.

. . .

In the center of a high-altitude gale, the Green Vine Immortal Sword flew over a distance nearly the size of an entire province. On its scabbard were the eight characters, “Spirit nurtures the green vine, the sharp edge deeply concealed,”6 one of which had already disappeared in the flowing stream of light. A surging sword intent permeated the entire body like a solid substance, tearing apart all the surrounding winds within ten feet.

. . .

In the demon cave, the premonition that arose in her heart was unprecedented in intensity, making Madam Hong restless, to the point where she no longer cared about her reputation. She left her alcove and sat down at a stone table in the central cave hall.

“Come out, I have something to ask you!”

Madam Hong shouted. After a while, voices came from all directions of the cave.

“Haha, Madam Hong has recovered?”

“I don’t think so!”

“What’s the matter?”

Three demonic auras filled the air, and three figures entered the cave hall, glancing at Madam Hong, who seemed to have recovered.

“Hmph, I will be responsible for getting the blood food for the next season. Now I want to ask you something.”

As she spoke, Mrs. Red recalled the events of the previous night and wondered:

“Have you ever heard of a certain kind of flame that does not burn tables, chairs, wooden beds, curtains, cloth, stone, or mud, and has a grayish-white tint to its color?”

The three demons had seen Madam Hong’s injuries before, but they didn’t get a good look at her before she hid in her own room. Now, they were a little curious.

“There exists such a flame?”

“Some kind of True Fire?”

“But it doesn’t hurt like the real thing?”

The three demons spoke one sentence each. When she heard their final statement, Madam Hong raised her arm with a sneer. Her silk sleeve slipped down to reveal her right arm, which was still charred black.

“Would you call this not hurting like the real thing?”

For a moment, none of the demons could figure out what was going on.

Another female demon covered her mouth and smiled.

“Could it be that Sister Hong is afraid? Do you think you have offended some immortal master who shouldn’t be provoked? After hiding in this Dazhen for all these years, only Yuhuai Mountain could be regarded as an immortal residence. How…”

Shiiiing…

At this moment, as if a silver string were ringing in the heavens, the sound of a sword being unsheathed suddenly rang out, penetrating through the mountain wall and passing straight through the cave.

When the four demons heard this sound, they seemed to remember the horror of trembling in fear at the sound of thunder on a stormy day when they were just little evil spirits.

Chills ran down their scalp, and almost by instinct, the four demons dodged in all four directions of the cave hall as a monstrous aura filled the air.

Swish…

In that next moment, silver light filled the hall under the very eyes of the three demons.

“Execute!”

A voice as majestic as heaven and as calm as a murmur followed the light…

In just a few breaths, the demon cave was no longer dark. A large hole more than ten feet in diameter had been punctured into the stone mountain cliff above, and the afternoon sun shone in.

Madam Hong’s demonic aura had already melted away like snow.

The three transformed monsters raised their heads with trembling faces. Tens of hundreds of feet above in the sky, an unsheathed immortal sword pointed its blade toward the bottom of the Dark Wind Ravine. The sword’s edge was so sharp, and its sword intent was so powerful, that even the scorching sun seemed inferior!

T/n: RIP Madam Hong, here so quickly, destroyed so thoroughly, gone so soon

黑 (hēi): black/dark/sinister/illegal, 风 (fēng): wind/news/custom. This chapter is a play on words: 1) the name of the current location (Heifeng Ravine), 2) the power of the sword causing a wind to blow, 3) “evil wind” is another way to refer to evil spirits, and it’s making them tremble.

瓦 (wǎ): roof tile, 风 (fēng): wind. Something like “Windy Peak Mountain”

See footnote 1. The ravine is pretty much called “Evil Windy Place,” is anyone here surprised that it turned into a hang-out spot for evil spirits? I hesitated for a while over whether to leave it as “Heifeng Ravine,” but a lot of the sentences and the chapter title played on the meaning of the name so I decided to break my custom of leaving names in Chinese and translate this one

Eyes (2), nose (2), mouth (1), ears (2).

掩 (yǎn): to conceal/to hide, 息 (xī): breath (used like “presence”)/to stop, 土 (tǔ): earth/dust, 遁 (dùn): to flee/escape, 符 (fú): talisman/mark/sign/symbol/seal

See ch. 104, footnote 2

Chapter 116: The Sword Has a Spirit, and Aids Spiritual Beings

High in the sky, the Green Vine Sword swept through an entire province of land. As soon as it had determined its targeted Qi, it immediately unsheathed, and executed the demon in one blow.

When the thousand-zhang ray of sword light flashed, Madam Hong didn’t even have time to react before she was completely destroyed.

At this moment, the scabbard hung suspended beside the Green Vine Sword, and the Green Vine Immortal Sword was still floating high up, pointing at the three demons below. No, it was four demons, to be exact; somewhere in the demon cave, a half-human-faced demon was hiding in fear.

Although the most powerful sword strike had already been swung, the invisible sword intent of the Green Vine Sword still filled half of the sky, overwhelming the sunlight, and making the four demons below look like little spirits facing thunder from the heavens. They dared not move at all.

It felt like if they so much as thought of moving, even a little, that murderous intent would be directed towards them. The lesson learned by Madam Hong was still fresh in their minds.

The Green Vine Sword in the sky was also a little puzzled now.

The master only asked it to kill the demon who had been burned by the True Fire of Samadhi, but it did not inform it that, after it had split the mountain with one swing and killed the demon, several more demons would appear.

Of course, at this moment, the Green Vine Sword had already struck with its most powerful slash, and it clearly remembered the words of its master that it should not linger in the battle. However, the demons below didn’t dare move when they saw it, so there was no need for it to rush away; it was not afraid of its own aura!

However, although the demons below were scared, they still calmed down somewhat from their frightened state. At least, this Immortal Sword did not seem to have any intention of killing them all at the same time, and its master did not seem to be nearby.

“None of us should move… That’s an immortal sword, and it was definitely aimed at Madam Hong. Apart from executing Madam Hong, its owner should not have given it any other orders, so the immortal sword is now suspended in the air…”

After all, even demons would sometimes miss their companions, or would be afraid that their companions would do something stupid and implicate themselves.

“Then, what should we do? How long will we wait?”

Another female demon asked in a low voice.

“Just wait! Wait until the Immortal Sword loses its patience and flies away by itself… or, Serpent-Woman,1 you can try to escape. I’m sure Hunhe2 and I will be grateful!”

Serpent-Woman didn’t even dare to get angry, for fear that her anger would trigger her demonic Qi and cause some misunderstanding with the immortal sword above her head. The man on the other side also said with a bit of hatred:

“What the hell kind of existence did this Madam Hong provoke? No wonder she called us out just now. She must have been too uneasy to be alone!”

“Oh, no! This immortal sword is so powerful, I’m afraid it has already been noticed by the City God!”

“I would rather fight with the City God and his ghosts than die under this sword for no reason…”

Perhaps because the three demons had spoken, the blade of the Green Ivy Sword high in the sky turned and pointed towards demon who had last spoken.

All three demons immediately seemed to have been hit by an immobilization spell, and stood frozen and mute. Their hair stood on end, and they dared not even breathe. The one named “Hunhe,” at whom the sword was pointing, had a stiff face and an expression even uglier than if he were crying.

. . .

Inside the capital city of Chunhui Prefecture, the golden body of the City God was still hanging high in the sky above the City God Temple. As he looked into the distance in the direction of Duming Prefecture, there was a flash of sharp sword light, and an unmistakable demonic aura in the air.

Although that distant place where it came from was on the very edge of Chunhui Prefecture, as the City God of the prefecture, he was not prohibited from entering that location, even though it was under the jurisdiction of a county’s City God. Although the two parties were not in a hierarchical relationship, the City God of a prefecture still had some prestige and authority.

The soul-calling bell in the City God Temple had been ringing for a while. Sixteen head officials of the twenty-four divisions had already gathered, as well as the daytime patrols, and the soul-catching messengers holding umbrellas over their heads to avoid the sunlight.

“Onwards!”

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture waved his sleeves, and with his incense power and the supernatural powers of the earthly gods, he led his subordinates onwards.

The City God of Duming Prefecutre had also made the same choice as the City God of Chunhui Prefecture. In addition, the City Gods of several large counties at the junction of the two prefectures sent their Dharma bodies over and ordered their subordinates from various departments to appear at the border of their jurisdictions. If necessary, they would even risk leaving their counties to fight together.

Wafeng Mountain itself was located on the border of two prefectures, and intersected many different counties. Divine light flashed to either side of the border in the two prefectures, and the City Gods of Duming Prefecture and Chunhui Prefecture revealed their Dharma bodies. After gazing at each other from a distance, they looked up at the sky.

An immortal sword with unparalleled sword intent hung suspended in the air, its sight turned toward the Dark Wind Ravine in Wafeng Mountain. The tip of its sword pointed at the four transformed monsters down below who dared not move at all.

Swish… Swish…

Three more divine lights flashed, and the City Gods of the three large counties in each of the two prefectures also led their subordinates here. Even the City Gods of those three nearby counties actually left their jurisdiction and their subordinates behind, and flew directly there, alone in their Dharma forms.

Now the situation suddenly became delicate.

Ghosts and gods gathered on Wafeng Mountain, and the four demons in Dark Wind Ravine were frozen stiff. These demons had considered countless bad situations in their lives, but this strange situation was beyond their wildest imaginations.

The Green Vine Sword did not move, nor did it sheathe itself. It hung in the sky together with the scabbard, ready to strike.

It seemed as if all the ghosts, gods, and monsters were waiting for the Green Vine Sword to move, some hoping it would leave while others hoped it would strike.

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture looked at the Dark Wind Ravine and the terrifying crack in the mountain above, and realized that this must have been the power of an immortal sword that he had felt before.

‘Could it be that the Immortal Sword came on the orders of its Lord and had already killed its target? And now it saw that there were still demons but did not have orders, so it deliberately suppressed the demons here? ’

Chunhui Prefecture’s City God thought so in his heart; no matter what, these demons must not be allowed to escape. As a City God, he could see the resentment and violence in the Dark Wind Ravine at a glance. These were definitely true evil demons.

As the City God of a prefecture, the City God of Chunhui Prefecture was the most powerful of the assembled City Gods. Now, he first bowed to the Immortal Sword in the air, then spoke without hesitation.

“My thanks to the Immortal Sword who has lent us the power to suppress the evil spirits here and aided us in subduing them today… Everyone, as City Gods of prefectures and county governments, we must capture these villains and destroy the evil spirits!”

As he spoke, his golden body glowed with divine light, and the power of incense and fire mixed with spiritual energy, rising in intensity. His subordinate, a head officer of the underworld, had already recorded and registered the description of the demons, giving them nowhere to hide under his jurisdiction.

“As it should be! Let us work together to eliminate these demons!”

“Soul-catching messengers of both prefectures and counties, cast a formation to lock down the soul!”

One by one, black umbrellas were raised to block the sunlight. Judge’s brushes, soul-striking whips, evil-binding books, Ruyi crowns…3 The instruments of the underworld were all put into action.

The power of the assembled ghosts and gods rose. They all used their incense power and spiritual energy to form a united force, shining with divine light.

The rocks and trees at the base of the Dark Wind Ravine shook. The four demons below could no longer bear it. Demonic energy rose from their bodies, and their monstrous roars sounded.

“Everybody flee!”

After shouting, he raised his demonic aura to resist the powerful light of the assembled ghosts and gods. Some demons even called up a demonic wind and fought against the light in an attempt to fly out of Wafeng Mountain.

However, the moment he left the mountain, the immortal sword above his head burst into sword light, as if it were about to strike, scaring the evil spirit so much that it stopped in its tracks.

“Hahahaha…Stay here!”

A pale golden chain flew out from the sleeve of Chunhui Prefecture’s City God and shot toward the evil spirit like a living snake, instantly entangling the opponent and throwing him fiercely into the mountain.

“Boom!”

Rocks exploded and trees fell on the the mountainside.

“Four-way Soul Locking Formation!”

Soul-binding chains shot up from all sides of Wafeng Mountain as the soul-catching messengers under the various City Gods worked in tandem. For a moment, a murderous, chilling wind picked up in Wafeng Mountain.

“I haven’t seen transformed monsters for a long time. I didn’t expect to see three and a half of them all at once. Today, I will refine your souls and transform them back into spirits!”

The Chunhui Prefecture City God seemed to want to vent all the negative emotions that he had suffered yesterday. He used his spiritual energy and the divine light from his incense power as if it were inexhaustible. Every time he struck with his divine light, the blow was extremely powerful.

A large number of soul-binding chains traveled through the mountains, and often a demon would be captured by at least a dozen chains if it wasn’t paying attention, causing its spirit to be come turbulent and easily struck by the divine light of the other ghosts and gods.

But the most deadly thing was not the attack of the ghosts and gods, but the fact that, in this kind of battle between demons and gods, the immortal sword that continued hanging in the sky had still not struck. Everyone knew that no one could withstand the might of this sword.

They had to fight with this distraction…

The first of the four demons below to give in was the half-human-faced demon, whose soul was pulled out of his body by dozens of soul-binding chains.

The Serpent-Woman had transformed into her original form and drilled into the mountain towards the depths of the demon cave, hoping to escape with the help of underground water veins.

“Where are you going!”

The arms and sleeves of the City God of Duming Prefecture kept extending, and his palms and fingers continued to grow larger. He almost followed the Serpent-Woman into the depths of the demon cave and grabbed the snake’s head.

“Hiss… ROAR… Red Skull, you slut, I hope you died a horrible death…!”

Boom… Boom… Boom…

The giant snake writhed wildly in the cave, causing the peak of Wafeng Mountain to shake.

…

People living in the the three counties near Wafeng Mountain could see dark clouds covering the peak, and constantly rumbling thunder.

蛇 (shé): snake/serpent, 姬 (jī): woman/concubine/female entertainer (archaic)

There are 3 “leader” demons, this is the name of 1 of the three. The others are Serpent-Woman and the snark guy

Ok so the word 如意冠 (rúyìtào) as a whole actually translates to “condom” but that is NOT what they’re using here… 如意 (rúyì) means “as one wishes,” and Ruyi is decorative object, either a scepter or a talisman, symbolizing power and good fortune

Chapter 117: Dream of the Underworld

It was gradually approaching evening, and cooking fire smoke was rising from the homes in the mountainside villages.

Some people occasionally glanced in the direction of Wafeng Mountain. The dark clouds still lingered, but the sound of thunder had weakened.

“The weather was really strange this afternoon. The sun was still out, but it was cloudy over Wafeng Mountain. The thunder continued for so long, but we still can’t tell how heavy the rain is…”

“Hey, isn’t that just what the weather is like in the mountains?”

A couple villagers chatted at the entrance to the village as they waited for their wives and mothers-in-law to call them back for dinner.

“It won’t rain tonight, will it?”

“I can’t say for sure. If the clouds don’t disperse after dinner, bring in the clothes that are drying outside.”

“Okay!”

The villagers outside were still chatting, but for the the demons in the mountain the situation had taken a sharp turn for the worse.

After the half-human-faced demon had its soul extracted, the second unlucky victim was the Serpent-Woman. Because she tried to escape into the mountain in a panic, her original body was pressed into the hole she had dug by the City God of Duming Prefecture.

Countless soul-binding chains whipped into her from behind, destabilizing the Serpent-Woman’s soul and detaching it from her body. Then, more soul-binding chains immediately bound her soul, and officers of the various divisions joined the soul-catching messengers in extracting it…

In the end, the remaining two demons had to face the two Prefectural City Gods and the other ghosts and gods alone, at the center of the large formation. They didn’t last long.

Just before dusk, the horizon gave off a sunset red glow.

In the depths of Wafeng Mountain, the mountainside near Dark Wind Ravine was littered with fallen trees and tumbled rocks. The dust from the impact to the mountain still hung in the air, and had not yet completely dispersed.

The battle between the gods and the demons in the two prefectures of Ji Province had come to an end. Two of the demons had their souls extracted and captured, while the other two were eventually beaten to death. It was difficult to say whether the latter had survived.

Above Wafeng Mountain, the Green Vine Sword sheathed itself in the air. The sword intent in the sky was immediately stored away, and the stars in the sky that were faintly visible grew a little clearer.

All the ghosts and gods seemed to sense the disappearance of the sword intent, and looked up at the immortal sword in the sky.

Without waiting for the ghosts and gods to react, with a quiet, sharp sound, the immortal sword turned into a stream of light and rushed to the sky, gradually fading away from the sight of the assembled spirits, and entering a strong wind in the heavens to be carried off.

“I don’t know which immortal might own this Immortal Sword, but thanks to its help, we were able to smoothly subdue these evil spirits today.”

“Well, where does the sword light lead to?”

“It must be somewhere far away.”

It was rare for so many city gods to gather together as they did now. Thus, they simply chatted for a while on the mountain.

Below, the ghost officials of the two prefectural governments and the chief officials of the Yin and Yang Departments went into the demon cave to investigate. They found the source of the resentment miasma, and discovered hundreds of white bones in the darkness. This infuriated the city gods and the officials of both prefectural governments.

However, the deceased were most likely abducted civilians from different provincial jurisdictions. Otherwise, the underworld could not possibly be unaware of so many mortals who had been killed by evil spirits, This also proved that this group of demons knew how to take advantage of the loopholes in the underworld structure.

By the time everything had finished and the sky had turned to night, the city gods of the two prefectures and their respective counties stood on either side of the border passing through the center of Wafeng Mountain and bowed to each other.

“Everyone, this matter here has concluded, and we should disperse. This group of villains is related to a case that happened in my city. This ‘Red Skull’ that the snake demon cursed out is the perpetrator. Why don’t we take them back to the Chunhui Prefecture underworld for interrogation, and then we can inform you as to the results?”

The City God of Chunhui Prefecture bowed to the city gods before him and to the left and right.

“City God Zhao, there’s not need to be polite. Chunhui Prefecture is the capital of Ji Province, so it’s most appropriate for you to taken them in for interrogation!”

The City God of Duming Prefecture spoke up in agreement, and the city god of other counties had no objections.

“Thank you, City God Li, and thank you to all the assembled city gods. Until we meet again!”

“Farewell, fellow city gods!”

“Until we meet again!”

This scene was indeed a rare sight. After the city gods bid each other farewell, they either flew away or moved away, leaving only a mess in the depths of Wafeng Mountain. Fortunately, very few people would approach the Dark Wind Ravine, let alone explore th bottom of the gully, otherwise they would have been scared to death just seeing the piles of white bones in the depths.

In the eyes of the villagers living near the mountain, the dark clouds from the direction of Wafeng Mountain finally dispersed shortly after dark, and the thunder completely subsided. They could see the starry sky behind the mountain peak, so they could continue to hang their clothes in the yard to dry without worry.

. . .

In the Guixiang Inn next to the Imperial Examination Hall in Chunhui Prefecture’s capital city, Yin Zhaoxian’s fever had died down, and he had woken up. Although it was still nighttime, he was nonetheless reading a book by the candlelight in the room in a somewhat uneasy mood.

“Ah… I’ve provoked a monster, what should I do… I can’t stay in Chunhui Prefecture forever, what will happen to my family, my reputation…”

If he were an ordinary person, the memory of the City God’s dream last night would have become blurred the next day when the sun rose. However, Yin Zhaoxian remembered it clearly.

In the dream, the City God of Chunhui Prefecture didn’t seem to know much about the origins of the monster. Yin Zhaoxian had finally gotten the opportunity to realize his ambitions, it was unacceptable to wind up being eaten by that monster for no reason.

‘I wonder if I can go to the temple and ask for a protective talisman? ’

In the afternoon, the magistrate of Chunhui Prefecture sent someone to see him and inquire about his condition. At that time, Yin Zhaoxian looked very depressed; others thought he was sick, but in truth he was just worried sick.

“If only Mr. Ji were here…”

Looking at the words on the book in front of him, Yin Zhaoxian still couldn’t concentrate. His vision seemed to gradually become blurry, and he grew dazed and began to doze off. Soon, he had fallen into a deep sleep, slouched over the table.

“Yin Jieyuan, Yin Jieyuan!”

Hearing someone calling him, Yin Zhaoxian raised his head, only to find two officials in black official robes standing in the room next to him. The style of their robes was a little strange, and there were even characters on their tall caps.

On one man’s hat was written: Night patrol, not daytime patrol.

The other man’s hat read: Manage the yin, not the yang.1

Although he felt that something was a little unusual, Yin Zhaoxian stood up, bowed in greeting, and asked.

“Who might the two officials be?”

At Yin Zhaoxian’s questioning, the two men who appeared to be officials cupped their hands and bowed to him in return.

“Yin Jieyuan, the Lord City God of Chunhui Prefecture invites you to join him! Please, Jieyuan, make this easy and come with us!”2

‘This is really bad! ’

Yin Zhaoxian was shocked.

“Ghost messengers, has my life come to an end?”

“Hahaha… Yin Jieyuan, don’t be afraid. It’s not that your life has ended but simply that the Lord City God wishes to invite you to join him!”

“That’s right, we will take you back later, don’t worry!”

It was good that he wasn’t dead. After calming himself, Yin Zhaoxian agreed to the ghost messengers’ request. As he followed them to leave, he experienced the miraculous feeling of passing through a solid door.

After leaving the inn and walking through the streets, Yin Zhaoxian soon felt as if he were walking through fog. He followed the underworld messengers step by step into the underworld.

For a mortal, this was definitely a special experience. Although it was like trying to spot flowers through thick fog, he truly caught a glimpse of the bustle of the underworld. He spotted many ghost messengers, and underworld judges reviewing official documents. He even heard the sounds of whips and screaming coming from a certain direction.

The scream that left the deepest impression seemed to come from a woman. It shrill and extremely frightening.

“Yin Jieyuan, that one over there that is screaming is a vicious demon. It has murdered may people, and the punishment is already far from enough.”

“I see, I see!”

“This way, please!”

Yin Zhaoxian didn’t dare to say much or ask any more questions. He followed the ghost officers to a large hall, which looked very similar to the main hall of the City God Temple in Chunhui Prefecture. The City God of the prefecture was sitting in the room.

Yin Zhaoxian didn’t dare delay his greetings and hurriedly cupped his hands and bowed to the City God.

“Ning’an County’s Yin Zhaoxian greets the Lord City God!”

The City God came down from his seat and led him to a seat to the side of the coffee table.

“Yin Jieyuan, no need to be so polite, please sit here!”

Looking at Yin Zhaoxian’s nervous expression, the City God went straight to the point.

“Yin Jieyuan, the demon who attacked you before has been completely destroyed, and all other affiliated demons have also been captured or killed. They can no longer harm anyone.”

When Yin Zhaoxian heard this, he quickly stood up, despite only just having sat down with trepidation, and bowed to the City God.

“Thank you, Lord City God, for getting rid of these evil spirits. Now I can rest assured!”

“Yin Jieyuan, no need to be so polite. We actually may need to thank you at this time!”

The City God spoke, and continued without waiting for Yin Zhaoxian to wonder for too long.

“Yin Jieyuan, you once said that you made a strange friend. When the demon tried to harm you last night, you were injured by some ability that he left behind. Does your friend carry a sword with him?”

Generally, immortal weapons that had given birth to spirits would also practice their own cultivation, and were unlikely to—and did not like to—be stored within spatial treasures. Therefore, Yin Zhaoxian might very well have seen the true form of the immortal sword, and so the City God asked him this question.

“A sword?”

Yin Zhaoxian thought about Ji Yuan’s daily life and shook his head.

“I’ve never seen him with one, but… my son once said that he saw Mr. Ji performing a sword dance, and the fallen leaves and flowers followed his movements. Watching his sword dance was like seeing the bright clouds and the rising sun, and it felt like flowers blooming and flowing water…”

Yin Qing was gifted with a bright mind and pure heart from a very young age, so when he saw Ji Yuan’s sword dance, he could more clearly feel the path that the man’s aura traveled. What he experienced was not merely the superficial appearance of Ji Yuan’s sword dance.

Yin Zhaoxian had never seen it, so it was difficult for him to recount it clearly, and he could only give a general description.

However, the City God’s expression grew much more solemn at his words. He had originally been 60% certain, but that had now become almost 90%.

“Yin Jieyuan, although your friend may already know something about this, and perhaps someone of his level may not care about this matter, if you meet him again in the future, please pass on this item to him on my behalf! “

Yin Zhaoxian watched as the City God handed him an ebony plaque the size of his little finger, with a black tassel attached to it. There were no pictures or inscriptions on any of its sides. He didn’t know what it was, but since it had been given to him by the City God, Yin Zhaoxian he naturally didn’t dare to neglect it. He took the small wooden plaque with both hands and answered respectfully.

“If I see him, sir, I will definitely pass it on to him!”

“Excellent, thank you, Yin Jieyuan. I have kept you here for long enough, it’s time to send you back!”

The City God excused him, and Yin Zhaoxian didn’t dare to stay in the underworld for any longer. He bowed repeatedly in farewell. The City God also bowed slightly in return, and the two underworld messengers brought Yin Zhaoxian back.

As he passed by a certain place, he glanced into the distance at a certain dark red area in the direction of the Punishment Division. From time to time, he could hear many noisy shouts and screams.

Suddenly, there was a sound like grinding teeth, or a stroke of a saw, and the sound of whipping, standing out from among the other noises.

“Ah ah… I’ve said it all, I’ve already said it all… Kill me… Ah ah…”

The woman’s clear, sharp, and shrill screams made Yin Zhaoxian tremble with fright.

“Yin Jieyuan, please don’t stop, follow me!”

Yin Zhaoxian hurriedly caught up to the messenger under his urgent reminder.

“Alright, okay! Thank you for your help!”

. . .

In the inn’s room, Yin Zhaoxian was so drowsy that his head nodded off downwards. With a “boom!” it hit the table, and he suddenly woke up.

Looking around, he saw that he was still in the room at the inn, with a book in his hand.

“Was it a dream?”

Yin Zhaoxian was so puzzled, that he hadn’t noticed that he still held a cold ebony plaque in his right hand.

Chapter 118: Getting Into Trouble

[T/n: note on chapter title]1

On the other hand, in an inn in Juntian Prefecture, Ji Yuan, who was cultivating on the bed, felt a change in his heart. He opened his eyes and looked out the window.

About two breaths later, with a flash of light, the Green Vine Sword flew into the room and hovered in front of Ji Yuan, buzzing with excitement.

“Hum…”

Looking at the Green Vine Sword’s excitement, Ji Yuan smiled with relief. It seemed that the demon was successfully slain.

“Hmm? Did anything unexpected happen?”

The Green Vine Sword and Ji Yuan were in tune with each other, but Ji Yuan could only feel its emotions and understand the general idea, not its exact meaning.

“Oh, everything is settled?”

“Buzz…”

The Green Vine Sword seemed to twirl around as if seeking praise.

“Okay, okay, you are the best!”

Ji Yuan laughed. He didn’t know exactly what happened, but the sword had a spirit and could sense whether the problem had been resolved, perhaps even better than ordinary people could. It should be fine.

With that thought, Ji Yuan stretched out a hand and grasped the Green Vine Sword. He gently pulled out the blade, looked at it carefully, and found that the lingering, invisible word “execute” was only a little dim. There was still a mysterious yellow energy entwined with the sword.

“You didn’t use it all with one strike?”

Ji Yuan asked. He felt another proud vibration pass through the Green Vine Sword. It seemed that it truly did not need to unleash all of its power.

Ji Yuan pondered it for a moment, and finally agreed. He might have piled on a little too much before, which consumed a lot of the mysterious yellow energy in his inner world.

However, this is also very beneficial for the Green Vine Sword, so Ji Yuan did not lose out in the end.

After all, he had been greatly influenced by online culture in his previous life. No matter what he experienced or theorized, this mysterious yellow energy, which he suspected was based on virtue, was now gathered in the immortal sword. In addition to tempering and making up for the shortcomings of the original material of the Green Vine Sword, it also contained the potential for some kind of mysterious change.

Ji Yuan returned the sword to its sheath and placed the Green Vine Sword diagonally on the bed. Ji Yuan stretched his arms and looked at the stars outside the window.

“It’s time to sleep.”

He lay on his side on the bed, resting on his hand as the pillow. As he breathed in and out, he felt the spiritual energy and the five elements in his body flow along with his breath.

Ordinary people breathed into their chest, while warriors breathed into their abdomen;2 every time Ji Yuan inhaled and exhaled, his spiritual energy reached all the way down to the tip of his toes and flowed through his whole body.

Ji Yuan awoke naturally the next day when the rooster crowed. Because the concerns on his mind had been resolved, he slept very comfortably last night. He had also gained in his cultivation.

“Haha! Alright, it’s time to head out!”

Ji Yuan stood up, put on his coat, picked up the bag hanging by the bedside, and strode towards the door. The Green Vine Sword, which had been quiet all night, floated up, slanted its blade to follow Ji Yuan, and flew two arm-lengths behind him.

The door of the inn opened with a creaking sound. The innkeeper had left willow branches, a pinch of salt, and a face towel on the stool outside the door.

However, Ji Yuan was now free of dirt; except for occasionally wishing to splash some clean water on his face to refresh himself, he no longer needed to brush his teeth or wash his face.

He went to the counter downstairs, checked out of the room, collected his deposit, and left the inn.

This Hong’an Inn was exactly the same inn as the one where Ji Yuan had previously stayed. He came here again hoping to see if he could retrieve his lost luggage.

However, three years had passed, so naturally his bag and umbrella were nowhere to be found. There wasn’t anything important, but the loss of the bamboo slips and the chess manuals was unfortunate. After all, they had been given to him by City God Song; now, they may have long been used for kindling.

Juntian Prefecture was still as lively as ever. As he walked around the city, Ji Yuan could occasionally smell a faint scent of osmanthus flowers, though he didn’t know which courtyard it came from.

He walked along the same street that the entrance of the inn had been on, all the way to the western city gate. Along the way, he bought over a dozen flatbread cakes and a bamboo jar of pickled vegetables, as well as an umbrella. Then, he left Juntian Prefecture’s capital without hesitation, striding off into the distance.

About six or seven miles out of the city, he opened his eyes wide and looked back. In the direction of the capital, Ji Yuan could see a sky filled with streams of rising energy. The colorful auras gathered above the city, forming clouds: emotions, desires, hatred and resentment gathered together, all of which was normal for a city.

With a chuckle, Ji Yuan’s walking speed began to change, as if the earth were shrinking beneath his feet. He even hummed a tune, not quite singing nor chanting.

“Searching along~ Singing along~ Traveling along… Searching for fate~; This world under heaven~ Down below~ This beautiful world… heading for virtue…”3

After experiencing the game of chess, Ji Yuan would often unconsciously visualize his inner world superimposed on the real world. He did the very same at this moment; he was not using any techniques as he moved, but his figure was ethereal as mist. The fusion between his mood and his inner world gave him the sense of being integrated into the world.

Originally, Ji Yuan considered going back to Desheng Prefecture first, but after thinking about it, he had another idea.

Previously, he had held the white chess piece and fought against the demon in the sky of his inner world, Ji Yuan noticed from the brief glimpse of his energy that that Yin Zhaoxian had a new kind of literary aura. In addition, he could feel the power of the City God’s roar even from afar, so he knew that Yin Zhaoxian was definitely not in his hometown of Ning’an County.

It was such a coincidence for them both to be traveling, and he had gained a new literary aura as well. Ji Yuan could guess without even thinking about it4 that Master Yin would definitely perform brilliantly in the provincial examination, so he would most likely go on to the capital of Zhili Province to take part in the metropolitan examination,5 or even the court examination.6

Since Yin Zhaoxian would have gotten onto the Laurel Announcement, he would first need to return to Ning’an County, and then he would set off to Zhili Province at an ordinary person’s pace. Even if he wanted to travel long distances by boat or by horse, he would have to set off immediately to make it to the distant capital of Zhili Province from Ning’an.

The country of Dazhen was an important piece in Ji Yuan’s general path for humanity. His good friend Master Yin was also a key figure. Sooner or later, he would have to go to the imperial capital of the country to determine the situation. In that case, Ji Yuan decided that he would meet Master Yin in Zhili Province next spring.

As it happened, this was also a good opportunity to visit the Tongtian River along the way. The second friend Ji Yuan had made in this life, and the first dragon he had ever met, was in the Tongtian River, though Ji Yuan wasn’t certain whether he was the River God, or if he just lived in the river.

. . .

It was noon on the second day after Ji Yuan left Juntian Prefecture. A tea stall with several horse-drawn carriages was parked to the side of the official road in up ahead. It seemed like a good place for passers-by to rest. Ji Yuan could not see it clearly from a distance, but he could tell that it was popular, and that there were many people inside.

The two shopkeepers, one older and one younger, were a little too busy, perhaps because many customers had just arrived. When Ji Yuan arrived at the tea stall, he saw them making tea and clearing the tables. A fire was burning in the stove so as to circumvent the need for running water.

“Sir, please find a seat and sit down. We will serve you right away!”

The older man who was making tea shouted at Ji Yuan.

“No rush, no rush, you guys are busy!”

Ji Yuan replied. He looked around at the 8 tables in the tea stall. Almost every table was filled with people chatting with each other, drinking tea, and eating cakes. Only two tables in the corner on the other end had empty seats, so Ji Yuan headed over.

To one side of the two tables were two women and a child. The child turned a tea cup upside down and used a chopstick to drum along the top, making banging noises. Meanwhile, the two women were eating snacks that didn’t look very appetizing while drinking tea.

On another side of the table sat a burly man with a bamboo hat. Ji Yuan didn’t think too much and walked to the large man’s table.

“Brother, might I please sit here and rest for a while?”

The burly man had seen Ji Yuan approaching. When he saw that he truly wished to sit at the table, he replied expressionlessly.

“Of course!”

“Thank you very much!”

Ji Yuan bowed to the large man and sat down. He leaned his umbrella against the table and waited for the shopkeeper to come serve him.

“If you would like, sir, you can go ahead and drink.”

The strong man pushed his teapot forward, pointed to the teacup on the table, and said to Ji Yuan.

“I’m just a little thirsty, so I will happily accept!”

Faced with a man who would look frighteningly fierce to ordinary people, Ji Yuan naturally felt no pressure. He took a teacup, and raised the teapot, and poured himself a cup.

“Hahaha… Sir, you are quite composed and carefree, you don’t seem like an ordinary gentleman at all. Seeing how you are walking alone all this distance on the official road, without any animals to help you travel, don’t you feel tired?”

Although the burly man’s tone was flat when he spoke, it sounded different to Ji Yuan’s ears.

He turned his head and looked at the other table. Apart from the child at the table who was still pounding tirelessly on the teacup, the two women who seemed to be wearing sturdy clothes were also looking at him. In the blur of his vision, there was a vague sense of familiarity.

Additionally, it seemed that the burly man’s words had triggered something. Ji Yuan felt that the aura of many people in the tea stall had changed.

‘What?7 I just went to have a cup of tea, but I somehow got into trouble? ’

The chapter title is 摊上事了. If you take each of those characters individually, it gives you something like “finishing matters at the vendor’s stand,” but taken together they somehow (??) give you “getting into trouble.” If anyone knows why, please let me know. I’m going to chose the latter for now.

For example, for singers, they tell you to breathe into your diaphragm/stomach.

T/n: I thought translating poetry was bad… try translating localized bad singing

Lit: “guess with just his toes”

会试 (huìshì): The next step after provincial exams: country-wide exams

殿试 (diànshì): highest level of exams. If the metropolitan exam lets you be an official in charge of managing the country (for example, budget management, receiving complaints, compiling/reporting data), then the court exam lets you be a court advisor to the emperor (for example, creating policies, passing laws, managing the army, etc.)
